notes-clifflab-rocklab.md · converted to HTML on 1 Nov 2026

Notes: clifflab + rocklab (groundwork for the Rust rewrite)

Read-only analysis, 2026-11-01. Sources: rocklab/ (working tree = HEAD), clifflab/ at commit 803ba4f (rockpile.py read via git show; another agent is editing it live), memory rulings (clifflab-rockpile, grass-rock-labs, rocks-faceted-stones-rework, overhang-faces-red, project_render_pipeline). Line numbers below are rocklab/rocklab.py (HEAD) and clifflab/rockpile.py@803ba4f; clifflab.py@HEAD where noted.

0. Lineage at a glance

File Status Approach
rocklab/rocklab.py (469 lines) stable since 2026-10-29; a hub: imported by dirtlab, meadowlab, waterlab, shorelab, clifflab ×3 convex half-space rock: hull body → facet planes → chip planes → grain
clifflab/clifflab.py (1162) superseded gen (rev 1–6 of DESIGN.md); still provides attach_base used by rockpile SDF-ish field: per-body depth map D(s,z) on a footprint outline → marching cubes
clifflab/rockmass.py (423) abandoned experiment recursive tilted-plane splits of a volume into cells → rockify each
clifflab/rockpile.py (1987) live generator (commits 58e8105 → 803ba4f) gravity-stack of prisms on a support heightfield → per-column plane dressing → per-face crack-network relief cut → seat/round/chip/grain → assembly
clifflab/iterate.py, contact_sheet.py review tooling simulated drybrush paint + pyrender views + reference crop

DESIGN.md documents only clifflab.py (depth-map, revs 1–6). The rockpile design lives in its module docstring (rockpile.py:1–38), the commit messages and the memory file. A Rust port should treat rockpile as the cliff spec and clifflab.py as a rejected-but-instructive alternative.

1. rocklab

1.1 Pipeline (per tile)

  1. build_tile (rocklab.py:384) → scatter (360): N = round(U(9,12)·squares/4) items built first, sorted largest-first, then dart-thrown (≤400 darts, rim RIM_MM=1 + R, clearance CLEARANCE_MM=1.5 + Ri + Rj); unplaceable items dropped. pose (377) adds random yaw.
  2. make_item (339): D = 6 + 9·U^1.4 mm; with p=0.25 a cluster of 2–4 stones (satellites max(D·U(0.45,0.9), 4 mm) at evenly spaced angles ±0.5 rad, distance (D/2+d/2)·U(0.35,0.6) → overlapping), batch_boolean(Add).
  3. make_rock (324): family ∈ {dome .35, slab .2, block .2, wedge .25} → body → facets → chips → grain; calls num_tri() to force manifold3d's lazy eval (byte-stable STLs).
  4. make_body (146): convex Manifold.hull_points of 14–22 base-ring pts (z=0), 60–120 body pts at t∈U(.05,.97), 1–3 top pts; radius = elliptical footprint, superellipse only for blocks (p=3 for block, else 2; aspect 1–2; random yaw) × profile(family,t) (127; all non-increasing, e.g. dome √(1−t^2.2)) × shrink-only jitter × angle-only lumpiness 1+Σ_{k=2..5} a_k cos(kφ+φ_k), a_k~N(0,1)·0.08/2. Wedge: centre drifts by U(.2,.45)·footprint as t grows. h = min(D·U(.45,.85), 9), slabs ×0.6, ≥2.5.
  5. cut_facets (187): 4000 seeded area-weighted surface samples; variable-radius blue-noise dart throw (r = rmin+(rmax−rmin)·U^1.6, FACET_R_MM=0.9–2.5 × √(D/15); accept if ≥(r+rj)/2 from all); skip base samples (n_z<−0.3). Each seed → tangent plane tilted U(0,14°) about a random tangent axis, offset jitter U(−0.15,+0.1); skip if n_z<−0.5; rock = box ∩ half-spaces (trim_by_plane). 30–165 facets, keeps 70–90% volume.
  6. chip_corners_edges (231): candidate planes at every vertex above 0.6 mm (p .45, normal = Σ incident face normals, depth U(.3,.9)·√(D/15)) and sharp edges (dihedral>18°, p .35, random point 20–80% along, depth U(.2,.55)·√(D/15)); shuffled; tilt U(0,25°); reject n_z<−0.5 or loss >3% volume; cap U{8..16} chips.
  7. texture (283): refine_to_length(0.25) (manifold, no T-junctions), fbm (dharmatiles.stone.noise) 4 octaves @0.45 mm × 0.12 mm along vertex normals; × (0.5+0.5·smoothstep(dist-to-sharp-edge/0.5)) [edge keep]; × smoothstep((z−0.15)/0.8) × [n_z>−0.3] [base fade]; trim_by_plane z≥0.
  8. Floor: dirtlab.dirt_relief(seed) heightfield at 4.0 mm via make_heightmap_solid ∪ exact 1 mm box; rocks dropped by SINK_MM 0.3 + DIRT_SINK_MM 0.25.
  9. ∪ DB base (dungeonblocks.make_base, select_peg_height(zmax)), export after the float32 + merge-by-position reload check (main, 443) — raises unless watertight.
  10. audit_overhang (408): area with n_z < −sin(45°+3°), z>0.05.

1.2 What makes it look good

  • Three scales: facets 2–7 mm (planes), chips 0.2–0.9 mm (planes), grain 0.45 mm (displacement). Scales 1–2 are planar so ridges stay crisp; grain halved near edges.
  • Mixed-size facets by construction: one facet per variable-radius blue-noise seed ⇒ a mosaic of sizes (fixes R2 "dice" and R9 "big flat faces").
  • Angle-only lumpiness: keeps the profile non-increasing yet ensures neighbouring tangent planes always differ (no merged mega-facets).
  • Size-scaled detail: √(D/15) on facet spacing and chip depth.
  • Shawn: "spectacular… I love your work" (2026-10-28). Rejected: shave-extreme-vertex chips (dice, 50–68% volume loss), fracture scallops (Worley ~1.5 mm: "horrible"), harder facet tilt 22–28°, flake scars, uncapped chips.

1.3 Printability

  • Convex ⇒ grounded; every plane n_z ≥ −0.5 ⇒ no face beyond 30° below horizontal; grain zero on down faces and at base ⇒ measured overhang 0.00–0.02 mm².
  • Union of convex parts keeps those guarantees (surface ⊂ parts' surfaces).
  • 0.3+0.25 mm sink ⇒ no floating/ seam gaps. No tile-edge clipping (rim inset).
  • float32 reload watertight check; determinism (seeded sampler — the unseeded trimesh sampler was a real bug).

1.4 Parameters (module constants, rocklab.py:29–89)

tile SQUARE_MM 35, SURFACE_MM 4.0, SINK_MM 0.3, DIRT_SINK_MM 0.25 · mass WIDTH_MM (6,15), WIDTH_EXP 1.4, ASPECT (1,2), HEIGHT_RATIO (.45,.85), HEIGHT_CAP_MM 9, HEIGHT_MIN_MM 2.5, SLAB_HEIGHT .6, FAMILIES, WEDGE_OFFSET (.2,.45), BASE_PTS (14,22), BODY_PTS (60,120), TOP_PTS (1,3), LUMP_AMP .08 · facets FACET_SAMPLES 4000, FACET_R_MM (.9,2.5), FACET_R_EXP 1.6, FACET_TILT_DEG 14, FACET_OFFSET_MM (−.15,.1), FACET_MIN_NZ −.5 · chips SHARP_EDGE_DEG 18, CHIP_MIN_Z_MM .6, CORNER_CHIP_FRAC .45, CORNER_CHIP_MM (.3,.9), EDGE_CHIP_FRAC .35, EDGE_CHIP_MM (.2,.55), CHIP_TILT_DEG 25, MAX_CHIPS (8,16), CHIP_MAX_LOSS .03 · grain TEX_EDGE_MM .25, GRAIN_MM .45, GRAIN_AMP_MM .12, TEX_BASE_FADE_MM .8, EDGE_KEEP_MM .5 · cluster CLUSTER_PROB .25, CLUSTER_STONES (2,4), CLUSTER_SIZE (.45,.9), MIN_STONE_MM 4, CLUSTER_DIST (.35,.6) · scatter PLACEMENTS_2X2 (9,12), CLEARANCE_MM 1.5, RIM_MM 1, DART_TRIES 400, OVERHANG_DEG 45. CLI: --seed --cols --rows --check.

Anti-pattern for the rewrite: consumers override these by mutating module globals (_rl_params context manager in rockpile.py:306; _rl.HEIGHT_MIN_MM = … in scree). A Rust port must make these a per-call params struct.

1.5 Geometry representations / libs

manifold3d (hull_points, trim_by_plane half-space cuts, refine_to_length, batch_boolean, rotate), trimesh (surface sampling, face adjacency/dihedrals, vertex normals), NumPy; heightfield floor via dharmatiles.core.mesh.make_heightmap_solid; value-noise fBm from dharmatiles.stone.noise. No voxels/SDF.

1.6 Relation to src/dharmatiles/scatter/stones.py and stone/

Forked, not shared. rocklab imports only stone.noise.fbm, core.mesh.make_heightmap_solid, bases.dungeonblocks, core.config, render. The library's scatter/stones.py (build_stone, StoneSpec, _weather_bites, _seat_rotation; stone/surface.blur_remesh, stone/finish.stone_relief, stone/cracks.engrave_cracks, stone/shape.round_edges) is a different "aged/smooth" family (hull → seat rotation → domain warp → blur-remesh via marching cubes → broadband relief). rocklab REQUIREMENTS §7 calls stones.py "a separate, smoother aged family". Duplications: rocklab.to_trimesh/to_manifold ≈ grasslab.to_manifold ≈ rockpile.to_mesh; rocklab.audit_overhang ≈ grasslab.audit_overhang ≈ rockpile.audit_overhang (three copies, differing only in the angle); rocklab's inline float32 reload check ≈ grasslab.stl_safe ≈ stone.solidify.survives_stl32/separate_pinches; _smooth01 ≈ clifflab.smooth01 ≈ rockmass.smooth01; rockpile.round_edges (3 tangent planes, manifold) is a name-collision with the library's stone.shape.round_edges (vertex-based). Circular lazy import rocklab ↔ dirtlab.

1.7 Declarative generalisation

A rock is a convex-polytope recipe: ConvexBody(profile=dome|slab|block|wedge, footprint=superellipse(p, aspect), lump=angular_harmonics(k=2..5, amp)) → PlaneCarve(seeds=blue_noise(r=…·√(D/15)), normal=tangent⊕tilt, min_nz) → PlaneCarve(seeds=corners|sharp_edges, cap, max_loss) → Displace(fbm, weight=edge_keep·base_fade·facing). Clusters = Union(Translate(rock) …). Scatter = Place(strategy=dart(largest_first, clearance, rim), pose=yaw). Every random draw should come from a derived seed hash(tile_seed, path, index); that also removes the "build order changes triangle order" fragility (num_tri() forcing).

2. clifflab (rockpile, the live generator)

2.1 Pipeline (build, rockpile.py:1716)

  1. Form — make_form (392). Grid GRID_MM 0.25. Target height field T(x,y). FORMS table (356): band forms (run, corner, canyon, escarpment) via _band (417): wall split into masses MASS_W_MM 6–15 by _segments (333), each with depth DEPTH_MM 8–20 / full-height part TOP_DEPTH_MM 5–10, a stepped lower tier (TIER_Z .3–.7 of height, 1–3 steps ≥ MIN_STEP_MM 6, depth changes 0 or ≥ MIN_STRIP_MM 4), Poisson gullies (5–7 mm deep, ≥10 mm wide) and buttresses (20–25 mm), and a standard end section (END_MM 4, END_DEPTH_MM 11, full height) so tiles join (S4). rings forms (plateau, massif, mound, spire, needles) via _rings (482): concentric tiers per centre, angular masses with notch/lobe p .12, RING_JITTER .15, RING_STEP_MM 3; needles get extra centres and a ragged shared footing. Per-form mass (1.15–1.4) scales stones (Shawn wants massive blocks).
  2. Zones — make_zones (1885): 1.5 gaussian "shattered zones" per square (σ 5–10 mm) along x, floor .25. Drives stone size (ZONE_SIZE ×1.45 quiet → ×0.6 shattered), crack density, shoulder probability, scree density.
  3. Stack — stack (554), the core "gravity" algorithm on support heightfield S (starts at ground). Loop ≤MAX_ROCKS 400: pick the lowest open cell (S + 1.5·noise) with deficit T−S > 1 mm; draw kind from KINDS (91: column/block/slab/odd/monolith with along/depth/height ranges, bridge tolerance, tilt cap); size × form.mass × low/high gain × zone; enforce WIDTH_APART .25 vs last stone; grow footprint _grow (695: axis-aligned rectangle grown row-by-row over cells ≤ bed+bridge, snapping to pocket edges within SNAP_MM 3.5 so leftovers stay rectangular) or _grow_turned (745: rings forms, rectangle facing the outline tangent ±8°). Too-small pockets (MIN_AREA_MM2 8, MIN_SIDE_MM 3.5) → _merge_crumb (757) into a neighbour, retry later (CRUMB_RETRY), or left as a notch. Ground stones may _jut (782) 1.5–5 mm out. Rest: least-squares plane through bed heights, slope capped at the kind's tilt, + TOP_TILT_DEG 6 jitter, raised to touch the highest support. Height: kind range, BLOCK_ASPECT × narrow side, STONE_H_SHARE .32–.55 × cliff height, finish to T if leftover < MIN_H_MM 3.5 / CAP_MIN_MM 6. Anti-coursing stagger: re-draw height while its top lies within STAGGER_MM 2 of neighbours' tops/joints within COURSE_MM 3 over >25% of the shared edge. Write top plane into S; record Placed(cells, z0=_sunk (SINK_MM 0.8 into rock), top=(c,a,b), D, bed).
  4. Columns — columns (889): union-find (_components 870) stones sharing ≥ COLUMN_SHARE .6 footprint; column poly = convex hull; _open_faces (916) = footprint edges ≥2 mm whose outside midpoint is unoccupied at 2+ of 3 heights (or on the tile rim).
  5. Core — _core (1609): grey-erode S by a 2 mm disc, drop 0.5 mm → heightfield solid; side slabs subtracted with fbm relief (_rim_relief 1629) ⇒ joints between stones are meant to show a dark recess. Not a guarantee yet: round 11 still had see-through ray holes (s3: 5, s4: 1, 2×2 massif: 262; round11/ray_after.txt).
  6. Column dressing — _plan_column (1020): on the column's convex prism, each open non-rim face (p .85) gets a plane turned 3–12° in plan and battered −3…+10°, taking ≤ DRESS_MAX_MM 2.5. _Cutter (989) accepts a cut only via _cleave (956: loss ≤15%, total keep ≥60%, ≥0.02 mm³), not undercutting support (> 1 mm³ of cones/core), and not raising _thinness (939: slice-and-open fin area at 5 heights, MIN_WALL_MM 1.2) by >0.4 mm².
  7. Face planning — _plan_faces (1168): coplanar adjacent open faces (≤25°, ≤3 mm, end-to-end) merged into one face so cracks run across joints; _plan_face (1207) → _crack_net (1068): cracks seeded at the barest spot (weight bare⁴), grown up & down by random walks (steps 2–3.5 mm, bend σ30°, wander ≤40°, never within 20° of level; 75% diagonal 25–50°, else near-vertical σ30°) until each end hits an edge, a bed joint (crosses with p .45), or another crack (T/Y); refuse <3 mm, meeting angle <35°, parallel-near-edge (welts). Rasterised (0.1 mm grid, exact shapely distances) into G (V-groove: depth 1.2–1.8, mouth 0.8–1.2, pinch .55–1.4 via 3 sines, end taper 1 mm, 40% fade-out) and F (50% of cracks: lip — upper side sunk 0.5–1.0 mm, eased over 1.5 mm).
  8. Per stone — rock_manifold (1344): prism of footprint buffered +0.03 mm (JOINT_OVERLAP_MM: avoid exact tangency) cut by top plane → _chamfer_corners (1678, vertical bisector cuts 0.8–1.6 mm → V seams) → column planes (shifted if stone stands back) → top-edge: loaded ⇒ 45° joint chamfer 0.3–0.55; open ledge ⇒ broken shoulder 0.8–2.5 (p .6·zone) → _seat (1488) if resting on rock → round_edges (1435: 3 tangent planes of a cylinder per edge >20°, R .35–.6, 45% crisp ×.35, in-column joints .15–.3) → rocklab chip_corners_edges with loss capped to 3 mm³ → _relief (1255) → _grain (1580) → _fit_tile (1415). - _seat: underside = max over outline points of cones at SEAT_SLOPE = tan(90−55+3)=tan 38° from (local bed + BEVEL_MM 0.6), clipped to local bed − slope·dist inside; bed pre-closed by grey closing (_seat_bed, SEAT_SPAN_MM 1.5) to avoid fins; near open faces over own-column stones only SEAT_KEEP_MM 1 deep. Built as heightmap solid, intersected. ⇒ no flat ceilings by construction. - _relief: ray-cast (trimesh ray.intersects_location, multiple hits) a 0.1 mm grid along −m to get front/back depth per (u,z); h = F + w·G (w from facing-ness, inset ramp); wall-thickness guard (≥ CRACK_WALL_MM 1 to back); row clamp top-down: kept[r] = max(front, min(cut, kept[r+1] + tan(47°)·g)) (overhang limit − 8° margin); EDT-extend past outline; two-level blur (0.08 crisp, 0.3 where clamp/outline moved it); build heightmap solid, transform into the face frame, rock − hm. - _grain: fbm 3 octaves @ FACE_GRAIN_LEN_MM 1.4, amp FACE_GRAIN_MM 0.25, one field over the tile (runs across joints), isotropic (stretched grain = "cloth folds"), Laplacian-smoothed normals, halved near sharp edges (cKDTree), zero on down faces and below seat. - _fit_tile: stones crossing the tile boundary are scaled along that axis to touch it (gap 0.02) — never clipped ("clipping is bad"); RIM_SETBACK_MM 0–1 for rim columns.
  9. Core gets the same _relief (pad 0). pile = batch_boolean(rocks + core).
  10. Ground — foot_ground (1773): dirtlab relief + concave talus bank h·(1−d/r)^1.6 (1.5–3.5 mm high, 3–6.5 reach by zone, fixed at ends for S4), capped at 0.25× neighbouring rock (grey dilation), dirt faded flat 3 mm from tile ends.
  11. Scree — scree (1800): foot cells (EDT 0–0.5 mm from rock) with outward gradient; 55/square rocklab rocks D 1–6 (U² skew), 2.5/square _block_stone (1867: box cut by 2–4 planes, rounded, chipped, grained) 4.5–8 mm, 45/square grit 1–2.2 mm buried .2–.5·D; families block/wedge/slab only (domes read as spikes); reach Exp(4 mm); lean in .35·D; gap .38·(Di+Dj); tilt ≤8°; sunk 0.3 + tilt lift below min ground under footprint.
  12. Dirt on tops — _dirt_layer (1897): ray-cast pile top heightfield; plug slits < 1.5 mm wide/3 deep (grey closing, away from steps); fill lows by grey closing 3 mm up to 1.5 deep; film 0.1 + 0.5·dirt relief; zero at lips/steps (>0.5 mm per cell or slope >35°) with 1.5 mm ramp; none on tops < 5 mm above foot; underside = eroded rock top − 0.3 → heightfield solid − under-solid.
  13. Assembly/export — union all, ∩ tile box, clifflab.attach_base (clifflab.py:1025: ∪ DB base, simplify(SIMPLIFY_TOL_MM), keep largest component, grasslab.stl_safe); on RuntimeError retry with coarser simplify tolerances EXPORT_RETRY_TOL_MM (0.015…0.1). Audit audit_overhang (1960) n_z < −sin(58°).

2.2 What makes it look good / Shawn's rulings

  • Structure from a real process: gravity stacking toward a target profile gives tight joints, stones bridging stones, tiers/gullies/buttresses — reads as a pile of real masses (refs B/H/N/C/M). The earlier depth-map Voronoi (clifflab.py) could only give "a crack network on a flat wall" (rev 6 regression, compare-rev6.png).
  • Columns as the unit of dressing: planes chosen once per column and cut through every stone ⇒ faces run across bed joints; joints read as grooves not cushions.
  • Anti-pattern rules (C4 masonry / C10 scale markers): stagger heights, width-apart, varied aspect per kind, no capstone rows, zones for clustered detail, size large-low/small-high.
  • Joined crack network (barest-spot seeding, walks ending on edges/joints/cracks, lips) — score plateaued ~5/10 vs ref N; N's "crisp flat sub-faces + narrow dark cracks" vs ours "crumpled" is still open.
  • Rulings: straight top edge is intended; ceilings must be fixed by construction (seat); overhang limit 55° from vertical ("45 is too conservative"); never clip at tile edge — shrink; seams between stacked stones must read as grooves; S4 end matching = a standard end section, a few mm mismatch OK; massive blocks wanted; round forms' stones face the outline tangent ±small yaw; Voronoi "flake" chips: hated, reverted; fine texture not reading at print scale is still open; stretched grain = folds (keep isotropic, strong); cracks hugging edges = upholstered welt. "spectacular… the best cliffs I've ever seen" was said of the clifflab.py rev 3 depth-map version; rev 6 then "regressed" → rockpile.
  • Every render paints overhang faces red (iterate.paint, n_z < −sin(OVERHANG_DEG+3°)).

2.3 Printability guarantees

Concern Mechanism
Overhang (55°) half-space cuts with no down-facing normals beyond limit; _seat underside cones at 38° slope (3° margin); _relief per-row clamp at 47°; round_edges skips planes with u_z < −0.5; grain zero on down faces; _support_cone (1660) wedge; audit n_z<−sin 58°
Flat ceilings _seat (by construction; replaced keel 2026-10-31); _seat_bed grey closing
Thin walls/fins _thinness gate on every cleave; footprint opening by MIN_WALL_MM 1.2 (footprint_polygon 811); CRACK_WALL_MM; relief outline blur; crack mouths ≥ 0.8 (nozzle 0.4); dirt slit plugs
Islands/holes hidden core under the pile; stones sink 0.8 mm into supports; scree sunk below min ground
Watertight / float32 JOINT_OVERLAP_MM 0.03 instead of tangency; _cleave min 0.02 mm³ (no slivers); stl_safe (pinch separation + float32 merge + single body); simplify-retry loop
Clipping _fit_tile scales back to touch boundary; only the final ∩ tile box as safety
Determinism single default_rng(seed) threaded through + separate stream [seed, 11] for face planning so stones don't move when crack code changes

2.4 Parameters

~150 module constants, rockpile.py:71–264 (grouped: form, stack, finish, crack network, assembly) plus FORMS/KINDS/BLOCK_ASPECT/CORNER_KINDS tables (see §2.1 for the load-bearing ones). CLI: --form {run,corner,canyon,escarpment,plateau,massif,mound,spire,needles} --seed --cols. Per-form overrides: height (scalar or ramp pair), cap, mass, kinds, depth, top_depth, buttress, rings, centres, base.

2.5 Geometry representations / libs

  • 2-D grids (0.25 mm): target T, support S, footprint masks, beds — scipy.ndimage (EDT, grey closing/erosion/dilation, gaussian, label).
  • 2-D vector: shapely footprints (union of cell squares → buffer/simplify/opening), crack polylines, exact distances, line_locate_point.
  • Mesh CSG: manifold3d (extrude CrossSection, trim_by_plane, hull_points, booleans, slice, refine_to_length, simplify, transform/scale).
  • Heightfield solids for complex surfaces then booleaned in: seat underside, relief cut (in face frame), core, rim relief, dirt layer, ground (make_heightmap_solid, adaptive error_threshold).
  • Ray casting (trimesh/embree): face depth for relief; top heightfield for dirt.
  • Vertex displacement: grain (fbm value noise).
  • clifflab.py (old): implicit field f = max(G−z, min(d−D(s,z), P−z)) on 0.18 mm voxels → skimage marching cubes → quadric decimation; voxel support backstop.
  • Render: dharmatiles.render (pyrender), simulated drybrush paint (Laplacian-smoothing cavity/convexity → palette).

2.6 Mechanisms: lab-specific vs general

Mechanism Where Classification
Value-noise fBm relief/grain stone.noise.fbm; rocklab texture, rockpile _grain, _rim_relief general (displace-along-normal with weight masks: edge keep, base fade, facing) — duplicated twice
Half-space plane carving with acceptance tests rocklab facets/chips, rockpile _Cutter/_cleave, _block_stone, round_edges general "carve(plane, guards=[max_loss, min_keep, no_fin, no_undercut, min_nz])"
Variable-radius blue-noise / dart throwing rocklab cut_facets, scatter; rockpile scree general placement primitive (also in library scatter/distribute.py)
Gravity stacking on a support heightfield rockpile stack, _grow, _merge_crumb general-in-disguise ("fill volume T with packed primitives under gravity"); reusable for rubble walls, piles, ruins
Seat construction (underside from bed at the overhang slope) _seat, _seat_bed, _support_cone; rocklab sink general: any object resting on terrain/other objects
Edge rounding by tangent planes round_edges (rockpile) vs stone.shape.round_edges (lib) general; two incompatible implementations
Crack/fracture network (random walks ending on features) _crack_net general 2-D "line network" generator; library has stone.cracks.engrave_cracks (mesh walks) and fieldstone's crack tessellation — three variants
Voronoi / anisotropic warped Voronoi cells clifflab.py voronoi, level general (rejected for cliffs but used by walls/fieldstone)
Profile-relief cut via heightmap in a local frame _relief, _rim_relief general "relief on a planar face" op (texture any planar face of a solid)
Overhang clamp along a scan direction _relief row clamp; clifflab.py clamp_up general FDM guard for any heightfield/depth map
Dirt by morphological closing _dirt_layer; also dirtlab general "accumulate material in lows" op over a top heightfield
Heightfield solidification make_heightmap_solid everywhere general, already in library
Union-find grouping (columns, coplanar faces) _components general utility
Zones (1-D clustered intensity field) make_zones general "activity field" (clifflab.py used rank-transformed 2-D noise for an exact 60/30/10 split)
Talus bank / scree by distance from rock foot_ground, scree general "apron + debris near obstacle"
Fit-to-tile by scaling _fit_tile general tile-boundary policy
Export guarantees (float32 reload, pinches, single body, simplify retry) stl_safe (grasslab), attach_base, rocklab main general, triplicated (library stone/solidify.py too)
Overhang audit + red paint audit_overhang ×3, iterate.paint general diagnostics
Paint simulation (cavity/convexity drybrush) iterate.paint, contact_sheet.vertex_paint general eval tool, duplicated twice
Forms table (band/rings target profiles) FORMS, _band, _rings lab-specific content, but a natural declarative target

Cross-lab coupling: rockpile imports dirtlab (dirt_relief), grasslab (to_manifold, stl_safe via clifflab), rocklab (chip_corners_edges, make_rock, texture, _smooth01, constants), clifflab (attach_base), and mutates rocklab globals (_rl_params).

2.7 Declarative generalisation

The cliff decomposes cleanly into composable definitions:

Region(cliff):
  target   = Form.band(walls=[back], height=35, masses=…, tiers=…, gullies=…, buttresses=…, end_section=…)
           | Form.rings(rings=[…], centres=…, base=…)
  fill     = GravityStack(target, kinds={column: Kind(p, along, depth, height, bridge, tilt, aspect)…},
                          rules=[stagger(2mm, 25%), width_apart(.25), min_piece(8mm², 3.5mm), crumb=merge|notch],
                          size_field=zones, mass=1.35)
  group    = Columns(share=.6)              # union-find
  per column:  Dress(plane: yaw 3–12°, batter −3–10°, max 2.5mm)
  per face:    CrackNet(density, walk params, lip, groove) → FaceRelief
  per stone:   ChamferCorners → JointChamfer|Shoulder → Seat(slope=overhang) → RoundEdges
               → Chips(rocklab) → FaceRelief → Grain(field=tile) → FitTile(scale)
  hidden   = Core(erode 2mm, drop .5mm, rim relief)
Region(foot): Ground(dirt) + Apron(by distance, zone) + Scatter(rocklab.Rock, near=cliff.foot, …)
Overlay:   DirtAccumulate(closing 3mm, film, lips=0)
Export:    Union → ClipTile → Base(DB) → Guarantees(float32, single body, overhang ≤ 55°)

Key simplifications for Rust: (a) a single printability contract object (overhang angle, min wall, min groove mouth, nozzle) consumed by every op instead of scattered constants; (b) seeds derived per node path (hash(seed, "cliff/stack/rock[17]")), replacing the shared RNG stream and the [seed,11] side-stream hack; (c) one "carve with guards" op, one "relief on planar face via local heightfield" op, one "seat on bed" op, one "accumulate in lows" op — each reused by rocks, walls, piles; (d) per-call parameter structs instead of global overrides; (e) the many heightfield→solid→boolean round-trips are a performance hotspot (relief per face per stone) — a Rust port could do relief as direct mesh displacement in the face frame, or SDF evaluation, if the overhang clamp is preserved.

notes-grasslab-meadowlab.md · converted to HTML on 1 Nov 2026

grasslab + meadowlab — technical notes for the Rust rewrite

Read-only analysis, 2026-11-01. Sources: grasslab/{REQUIREMENTS,DESIGN}.md (rev 4/5, code v3 "stroke blades"), grasslab/grasslab.py (1189 lines), grasslab/blades_demo.py (108), meadowlab/{REQUIREMENTS,DESIGN}.md (rev 3, code v1e + v3 grass), meadowlab/meadowlab.py (266), git log -- grasslab meadowlab (17 commits, 2026-10-27…29), memory files grass-stroke-blades, grass-rock-labs, leaf-method-for-grass-rework, overhang-faces-red, project_render_pipeline. The labs' out/ hold only STL/3MF (no PNGs); the renders listed in §C were made from the existing v3 STLs into the scratchpad (overhang faces painted red per policy).

0. Relationship map

dirtlab.dirt_relief ──► grasslab ──► meadowlab ◄── rocklab (scatter, pose)
                          │  ▲
                          │  └─ dharmatiles: core.mesh.make_heightmap_solid,
                          │      bases.dungeonblocks (make_base, select_peg_height),
                          │      core.config (BaseConfig, SurfaceConfig), render.render
                          ├──► shorelab (make_ground, make_direction, Swaths, place_lenses,
                          │      finish_flow, build_grass, Grid, Field2D, stl_safe…)
                          └──► clifflab/{clifflab,rockmass,rockpile} (to_manifold,
                                 stl_safe, audit_overhang only)
  • Labs are imported as modules via sys.path.insert (grasslab.py:30–32, meadowlab.py:31–35). Meadowlab is pure composition: it calls grasslab and rocklab functions with arguments (no monkeypatching since the 2026-10-27 refactor, commit 6d553a5), and owns only the interaction logic (~150 lines).
  • No relation to src/dharmatiles/grass/ or layers/grass_carpet.py. Neither lab imports them. The library's grass is the older segment-by- segment growth sim (grass/grow.py, FloppyGrassLayer), thatch.py (draped-blade experiment), and GrassCarpet (Gaussian noise base + flat tube blades from FlatGrassGrower, Voronoi-grouped seeds). grasslab is the greenfield replacement produced by the "leaf method" Shawn asked for (requirements interview → by-construction guarantees → render-judged iterations); per memory the labs are to stay standalone for now.
  • Duplicated code across labs (prime candidates for one shared Rust module):
  • _smooth01/smooth01 in grasslab:165, rocklab:100, dirtlab:132, shorelab:86, waterlab:229, clifflab:217, rockmass:86.
  • poisson_sites grasslab:213 vs waterlab.poisson waterlab:239 (and the library's scatter/distribute.py has its own Voronoi/jitter helpers but no Poisson-disk).
  • to_manifold grasslab:985 vs rocklab:111.
  • stl_safe grasslab:1060 vs dirtlab:529 (dirtlab's calls the library separate_pinches; grasslab has _separate_pinches grasslab:1026, a faster byte-identical reimplementation of dharmatiles.stone.solidify.separate_pinches).
  • audit_overhang grasslab:1086, rocklab:408, dirtlab:561, rockpile:1961 (all n_z < −sin(OVERHANG_DEG + 3°); grasslab 45°, clifflab 55°).
  • attach_base grasslab:999 vs meadowlab:181 (same slab-box + DB base + keep-largest-body recipe, slightly different simplify order).
  • self_check grasslab:1096 vs rocklab:416.

A. grasslab

A1. Pipeline (grasslab.py main :1116)

RNG: one np.random.default_rng(seed) stream consumed in a fixed order; several values are drawn and discarded purely to keep the stream aligned (hill noise drawn even when --flat :242; swath envelope amplitudes unused). Grid: Grid(W, H, vox) :203, vox = 0.12 mm (0.10 at 1×1), snapped so W/vox is integral (:1133).

  1. Hills H₀ — make_ground :238. On a 0.25 mm grid extended 12 mm past the tile: unit-variance Gaussian-filtered white noise σ 10 mm + 0.35 × σ 5 mm (_smooth_noise :183, mode='wrap'). Rises-only: subtract the interior 40th percentile; smoothmax(raw, 0, k=0.25·std) (:179, softplus form) minus k·ln2 so smoothmax(0,0)=0; clamp ≥0; scale so interior max = 7 mm; cosine feather over 9 mm to the 3.4 mm edge level (h = 3.4 + w(dist)·raw); slope cap — if max slope > 34° scale the relief uniformly (:264). Returns Field2D (bilinear sampler via ndimage.map_coordinates order 1, :188).
  2. Base flow — make_direction :311. Scalar angle field (cannot form singularities): - θ_r = U(0,2π) + 60°·n(σ 15 mm). - In-phase meander: _along_flow_coord :291 solves ∇s ≈ (cos θ_r, sin θ_r) in least squares = Poisson ∇²s = div(cos θ, sin θ) by FFT on a mirror-extended grid. θ = θ_r + A·sin(2π s / 15 mm + φ), A ∈ 10–30° from smoothstep(0.5+0.35·n(σ12)), φ = π·n(σ14). Because iso-s lines cross the flow, neighbouring streamlines wave in phase ("combed hair"). - Downhill pull: vector blend toward −∇H₀ with weight 0.3·clip(slope/tan 34°, 0, 1). → Flow :275 (unit vx, vy + samplers).
  3. Swaths — Swaths :336. Poisson-disk sites at 11 mm (+10 mm margin); axis = flow at site; ownership = argmin of anisotropic distance ‖(Δ·d, Δ·d⊥/0.65)‖ + 2.5 mm·warp(σ3)·φᵢ (per-site random phase so the shared warp actually bends borders) — a warped anisotropic Voronoi, total coverage by construction. Per-cell normalised coords s ∈[0,1] along, l ∈[−1,1] across (coords :388). In v3 only s is used (frond length × (0.8+0.3s)); the height envelope env :394 is dead but kept for the RNG stream.
  4. Soil lenses — place_lenses :442, Lenses :401. N = round(U(1.3, 1.8)·squares); 80 candidates inside a 7 mm rim inset scored by −zscore(H₀) + N(0, 0.8); greedy best-first with ≥ 13 mm separation. Shape: axis = flow at centre, half-length U(5,9), half-width ×U(0.45,0.65); eye metric f = |a|/ℓ + (b/w)² (pointed ends), approx distance d = (f−1)·w/2 + shared border warp (±0.8 mm, σ 1.2) (edge :418, dist :431); mask = smoothstep(0.5 − d/0.5 mm) (:438).
  5. Finish flow — finish_flow :467. Per lens (own band U(1.5,4.5) mm, strength U(0.55,1.0)): remove the inward normal component v ← v − w·min(v·n̂,0)·n̂; parting v ← v − 0.6·w·min(v·n̂,0)·tanh(b/0.8)·p̂ (smooth tanh side choice — a hard sign() gave a herringbone seam); renormalise. Then steer_rim :527 on the angle: - Three smooth knob maps (ramp 9–15 mm, allowed angle φ_ok 70–88°, pull 0.15–0.45). - _rim_sides :492: per-perimeter-sample band-weighted mean lean sin φ laid out as one CCW loop, Gaussian-smoothed σ 20 mm along the loop across corners; its sign = turn side (one per rim stretch); hard (σ 2.5) and soft (σ 8) versions. - Per edge, _turn :511 compresses inward headings to ±φ_ok on the rim side, pulls already-outward headings further out, squares up around a side flip (pull ≥ 0.85·(1−|soft|), ramp ×2), runs alongside within 3 mm. - Corners: soft nearest-edge partition of unity exp(−(d_e−d_min)/2 mm). - Applied as θ + G_σ1.5mm(Δ): a continuous scalar rotation, so no new singularities (starbursts) can appear.
  6. Surface — Surface :673. On the 0.12 mm grid: seat H₀ − 0.4 mm × blur(soil mask, σ 0.8) (soft trough under clearings); H0_dirt = H0 + dirtlab.dirt_relief(seed derived from ground sum, no RNG); EDTs: soil_depth, soil_dist; optional blocker (meadowlab rock footprints) EDT blk_depth and side factor near (:704).
  7. Strands & spines — make_strand_roots :645, plan_fronds :733. Poisson seeds 2.65 mm (+8 mm margin); each strand U(15,30) mm long, starts U(0.2,0.6)·len upstream (backward RK2 trace) then forward trace; roots every 0.5·L·U(0.8,1.2) with L = U(3.5,6.5), ±0.3 mm lateral jitter. Per root: swath coords; bank flag (within 4 mm of soil, not in it); heading offset — strays 5% (12% on banks) ±U(20°,35°), else U(±4°), banks +U(±15°). trace_batch :621: vectorised RK2 midpoint streamlines, unit speed, 0.25 mm steps, all roots at once, rotated by the root's offset.
  8. Frond draw & shortening — draw_frond :765. Skip roots under a blocker; soil roots become sprigs w.p. 0.10 (L 1.8–3, half-w 0.5, h 0.6) else dropped. L = L0·(0.8+0.3 s_b); half-width U(0.8,1.25); crest h U(1.5,2.0) (×U(0.65,1.15) on banks). Per-frond endings: rim tip inset 0.2+1.3·U², soil spill 0.8+2.4·U² (4% wisps 4 mm). Shorten, never slice: walk the spine, stop at the first sample outside the rim inset / deeper than spill into soil / deeper than tuck_mm into a blocker; drop if < 1.5 mm; shortened fronds lowered h·clip(L/L0, 0.3, 1)^0.8.
  9. Blade strokes — blade_layers :812 (the heart of v3). N = round(h/0.2) layers. Per layer k: - line count n_k = round(n_max·sin(π(k+½)/N)^0.6), n_max = clip(round(2·half_w/0.45), 2, 4); n_0 ≥ 2 (keel), n_top = 1 (ridge), n_k ≤ n_{k−1}+1 (≤ ½ line per side per layer ≈ 45°). - back end u_back = L·(1 − (1 − (k+½)/N)^{1/2}) (convex crest 1 − (1 − u/L)²). - front end u_front = L − (N−1−k)·0.2 mm (RAKE_MM): only the top layer reaches the tip → raked bow, tip overhangs air to the last layer. - stroke = union over j of shapely LineString(spine[u0:u1]).buffer( (n−j)·0.45/2) with u0/u1 shrinking by 0.45 mm per line (TIP_STEP_MM) → nested round-capped buffers → both ends taper to a single line's round end (points in plan).
  10. Smoothing — blade_mesh :860. Each layer polygon closed by 0.3 mm (morphological close via ±buffer) → rasterised (shapely.contains_xy) on a 0.05 mm grid → 2-D signed distance by two EDTs. 3-D field in height-above-ground h: linear blend between layer centres (a loft); above the top centre the field closes ½ line over ½ layer (rounded ridge); below the bottom continues 0.3 mm (EMBED_MM) into ground. Gaussian blur σ 0.05 (xy) / 0.15 (h); skimage marching cubes at 0.05 mm; each vertex z += bilinear H₀ under it (blade drapes on hills); trimesh quadric decimation to 1,500 faces. Blades are meshed on a 4-thread pool (_pool :610) — drawing uses RNG, meshing doesn't.
  11. Grass top map T (build_grass :964): each layer footprint raises T to H₀ + (k+1)·0.2 — used for DB band check, gap statistic and (meadowlab) rock emergence.
  12. Ground solid & union — ground_solid :945, build_grass :951. H0_dirt → make_heightmap_solid (library) from z=0 → Manifold; Manifold.batch_boolean([ground] + blades, Add).
  13. Base — attach_base :999. ∪ exact 1 mm slab box ∪ DB base (peg height from tallest point) → simplify(0.005) → decompose() keep largest volume (crumbs + negative-volume internal voids dropped = voids filled).
  14. Export — stl_safe :1060 then STL; stats print; optional self_check :1096 (3 monochrome pyrender PNGs: iso, top, central 18 mm close-up).

blades_demo.py: same pipeline on a flat 16 mm slab with no lenses, picks the N roots nearest centre, writes either smoothed blades or the raw stroke stack (stroke_stack :29: each layer polygon → manifold3d CrossSection → extrude(0.2+0.01)), and reports printed_fraction :44 (area surviving an opening by 0.95·½ line = the slicer's view; ≥ 99.9%).

A2. What makes it look good

  • Mid-scale organisation first ("build the illusion, not botany"): combed ribbons that meander in phase, part around eyes, and lap rims; blade detail only decorates it. Visual scales table REQ §1.1.
  • In-phase meanders via the FFT-Poisson along-flow coordinate — the single most distinctive trick (lodged-grass photo look, no "hair" F1, no "commas" F24).
  • Scalar angle field everywhere until lens wrap; rim steering is a smoothed scalar rotation ⇒ no vortices/starbursts by construction.
  • Strands (long streamlines carrying shingled roots) give the dark seams between ribbons; ≈ 9% dirt shows between blades.
  • Blades on dirt, no turf mass; undercut tips over open air → dark shadows survive drybrush. Lit crest = 1-line ridge.
  • Shorten never slice; shortened fronds also lower ⇒ fringes step down.
  • Flow-aligned eye lenses with spill/wisps/sprigs, bank fronds with wander/strays/height variation ⇒ banks read as piles of blades.
  • Variation drawn from smooth knob maps (nmap) and U² skews, not white noise.

Shawn's rulings. Loved: "spectacular job"; flow ribbons; meandering look; v3 stroke blades ("make that the only way to generate grass blades"). Rejected (do not reintroduce): turf mass/skirts/pads; voxel FDM backstop; height-field fronds with carved lips; Catmull-Rom layer interpolation (lumpy); < 1,500 faces/blade (faceted); per-frond sickle bends; per-frond rim steering; swath-axis fans; global soft ends (fish scales); puddle soil, seam-lane soil, prism pebbles; hills dipping below the edge; one single sweep; size-scaling bare earth; full bare rings. Tune via stroke rules (line counts, RAKE_MM, TIP_STEP_MM, RISE_EXP), never by carving. Named failure modes F1–F26 (REQ §3) form an excellent review vocabulary.

A3. Printability guarantees

Guarantee Mechanism
Nothing narrower than a line Blades defined as whole 0.45 mm lines (blade_layers); verified by printed_fraction opening test
≤ 45° overhang per layer on the sides n_k ≤ n_{k−1}+1 (≤ ½ line/side/layer)
Tip overhang to the last layer but bounded rake 0.2 mm/layer (< ½ line)
Blades fused, no islands EMBED_MM 0.3 below ground; union with ground; decompose keep largest
Grass height bounded crest 1.5–2.0 mm × factors; limit 3.5 mm (REQ-B-7); measured maximum 2.20 mm (REQUIREMENTS.md:299)
Ground printable slope cap 34°, rises only, feathered constant edge
DB compatibility library DB base; mean top in 4.4–5.9 mm band (checked, not enforced)
Watertight after STL reload exact slab box for a clean base face; stl_safe (pinch separation, float32 merge, drop zero-volume fins, raise unless one watertight body); simplify(0.005)
Internal voids negative-volume bodies dropped in decompose
Determinism fixed RNG order; dirt seed derived from data; thread pool used only for RNG-free meshing (to_manifold(original_id) hook for stable IDs); refactors verified by sha256
Audit audit_overhang (n_z < −sin 48°): 15.4 mm² (0.14%) on seed 1

A4. Parameters (module constants; full table DESIGN §3)

CLI: --seed 1 --cols 2 --rows 2 --flat --vox --check PATH. Key constants: grids FIELD_RES_MM 0.25, VOX_MM 0.12, BLADE_VOX_MM 0.05; hills HILL_RELIEF_MM 7, GROUND_EDGE_MM 3.4, σ 10/5 mm, HILL_PLAIN_FRAC 0.4, MAX_GROUND_SLOPE_DEG 34; flow COHERENCE_MM 15, THETA_STD_DEG 60, MEANDER_WAVELENGTH_MM 15, MEANDER_AMP_DEG 10–30, TERRAIN_FOLLOW 0.3; swaths 11 mm, aspect 0.65; lenses 1.3–1.8/sq, half-len 5–9, aspect 0.45–0.65, sep 13, rim keep 7, deflect band 1.5–4.5 / strength 0.55–1.0, parting 0.6/0.8 mm; rim steering (15 constants, :92–105); strands 2.65 mm, 15–30 mm long; fronds L 3.5–6.5, half-w 0.8–1.25, h 1.5–2.0, stray 5%; strokes LINE_W_MM 0.45, LAYER_MM 0.2, KEEL_LINES 2, TOP_LINES 1, MAX_LINES 2–4, LENS_EXP 0.6, RAKE_MM 0.2, TIP_STEP_MM 0.45, RISE_EXP 2; smoothing close 0.3, σ 0.05/0.15, EMBED_MM 0.3, BLADE_FACES 1500; endings/banks; mesh SIMPLIFY_TOL_MM 0.005, SLAB_BOX_MM 1, OVERHANG_DEG 45, DB_GRASS_BAND 4.4–5.9. Hook: build_grass(…, blocker, tuck_mm=0.5, side_low=1.0, side_mm=1.0).

A5. Geometry representations & libraries

  • 2-D scalar grids (heightfield H₀, angle θ, vector flow, swath ownership, masks, EDTs) at 0.25 / 0.12 mm; bilinear Field2D.
  • Polylines (RK2 streamlines) → shapely 2-D polygons (buffered strokes) per layer = an explicit layer stack ("toolpath-aware" geometry).
  • Per-blade 3-D SDF voxel grid (0.05 mm), lofted from 2-D SDFs → marching cubes → trimesh → quadric decimation.
  • Heightfield solid via library make_heightmap_solid.
  • manifold3d for all booleans (batch_boolean, simplify, decompose, CrossSection.extrude in demo).
  • Libs: numpy, scipy.ndimage (gaussian_filter, EDT, map_coordinates), numpy FFT, shapely 2, scikit-image marching_cubes, trimesh (+ quadric decimation backend), manifold3d, scipy cKDTree (pinch finder), pyrender (self-check).

A6. Mechanisms (G = general mechanism in disguise; L = lab-specific)

Mechanism Where Class
Gaussian-filtered unit-variance noise, smoothstep knob maps (nmap) :183, :537 G (noise field)
Rises-only heightfield: quantile shift + softplus floor + feather + slope cap make_ground G (terrain primitive w/ printability constraint)
Least-squares along-flow coordinate (FFT Poisson) → in-phase modulation :291 G (field op: "phase coordinate of a direction field")
Vector blend toward −∇H :326 G (field combinator)
Obstacle wrap with tangential parting (remove inward component + tanh side push) finish_flow G (flow-around-obstacle op; reused by meadowlab for rocks)
Boundary steering of an angle field with per-stretch side choice steer_rim, _rim_sides, _turn G-ish (edge-condition op), but heavily tuned
Poisson-disk dart throwing poisson_sites :213 G (scatter) — duplicated in waterlab
Warped anisotropic Voronoi with per-cell local coords Swaths G (partition)
Greedy best-first scored placement with min separation place_lenses, rocklab scatter G (scatter)
Eye/lens SDF with warped border Lenses G (shape primitive "lens"), L params
RK2 batched streamline tracing trace_batch G
Strand → roots → fronds hierarchy make_strand_roots L (but = "instances along streamlines")
Shorten-never-slice against masks/EDTs draw_frond G (path clipping by predicate with height falloff)
Print-native stroke stacks (layers × whole lines) blade_layers G (toolpath-aware geometry; applies to any thin feature)
Loft of per-layer 2-D SDFs → blur → MC → drape on heightfield blade_mesh G (layer-stack solidification)
Height-map stamping of placed features (T) :964 G (same idea as library terrain_support_z)
Heightfield solid + batch union + slab box + DB base ground_solid, attach_base G (library + export)
Export guarantees (stl_safe, _separate_pinches, keep-largest) :1026–1083 G (dup across labs)
Overhang audit :1086 G (dup)
Monochrome self-check renders :1096 G (dup with rocklab)
RNG-stream alignment via discarded draws :242, swath env, soil_relief (removed) anti-pattern → fix with derived seeds

A7. Generalisation for a declarative language

  • Region = paint-bucket fill; grass is a layer stack on it: heightfield(rises_only{relief 7, plain 0.4, slope_cap 34}) → direction_field(regional{σ15, ±60°}, meander{λ15, amp 10–30}, follow_slope 0.3) → obstacles(lenses{per_sq…}, rocks…) (each obstacle declares a wrap band/strength) → edge_condition(rim_steer{…}) → instances(strands{…}, frond{…}) → geometry(stroke_blade{lines, layers, rake, tip_step, rise_exp}) → solidify(loft_smooth{vox 0.05, σ…, faces 1500}).
  • Direction field as a first-class value with combinators (angle noise, phase-coordinate meander, slope blend, obstacle wrap, boundary steer). Everything that "flows" (grass, water ripples in waterlab, stone grain) can consume it.
  • Obstacles as a uniform interface: anything placed (lens, rock, tree, wall) exports footprint mask, SDF, wrap(band, strength), blocker(tuck, side_low, side_mm). meadowlab's rock "circles" are really eyes (|a|/R+(b/R)², axis on flow0, M:135) and the wrap also needs the obstacle's axis and across-coordinate for the parting push (G:482–484) and uses the lens-metric gradient, not a true SDF gradient. So the wrap needs more than an SDF.
  • Derived seeds per definition (hash of seed + path name) replace the "draw and discard to keep the stream aligned" hack and the meadowlab two-stream fix.
  • Stroke primitive generalises: stroke(path, width_lines(z), start(z), end(z)) is a parameterised layer-stack body; blade = one preset; sprig = smaller preset. Smoothing is a separate op.
  • Measured constraints as declarative checks: mean-top-in-band, slope cap, overhang area, printed_fraction, watertight-after-reload.
  • Simplify: drop the dead swath envelope (keep only s for length scaling, or replace swaths by a low-frequency length noise along the flow); merge Surface's EDT bookkeeping into generic mask/distance fields.

B. meadowlab

B1. Pipeline (meadowlab.py main :207)

  1. Two RNG streams :216–217: grass seed, rocks seed + 1000003 (ROCK_SEED_OFFSET). Interleaved order: ground, flow, swaths (grass) → scatter, pose (rocks) → free lenses (grass) → aprons (rocks) → flow wrap, grass (grass).
  2. Ground, flow, swaths: gl.make_ground, gl.make_direction, gl.Swaths unchanged (:221–223); grid always 0.12 mm.
  3. Rocks first: rl.pose(rrng, rl.scatter(rrng, W, H, (6, 9))) :224 (rocklab: build items, dart-throw largest first with clearance, random yaw), so footprints are known before grass.
  4. Footprints — footprint_masks :62: Manifold.slice(0.45 mm) of each posed rock → polygons → matplotlib Path.contains_points on the grass grid (bbox-limited); footprint_mask :81 = union.
  5. Bare earth: - apron_shapes :86: 35% of rocks get a one-sided lens: half-width U(0.5,0.9)·R, stretch U(1.2,1.9), axis = flow, centred U(0.6,1.0)·R from the rock toward a side within ±90° of downstream (60%, wake) or upstream (scour). - Free lenses: gl.place_lenses(…, per_sq=(8, 8)) — large ranked pool (U(8,8) keeps the RNG stream aligned). - fill_bare_earth :115: add free lenses best-first at natural size until (soil > 0.5) ∪ footprint ≥ 20% on a 4× strided grid. combine :106 builds a gl.Lenses from first-k free + extra shapes, sharing the warp.
  6. Flow :231: gl.finish_flow on soil lenses plus rock_circles :129 (circle R + 0.6 mm as an equal-axis lens) — grass parts around rocks with no soil involved.
  7. Grass :233: gl.build_grass(…, blocker=foot, tuck_mm=0.5, side_low=0.5, side_mm=1.2) — no roots under rocks, spines stop 0.5 mm inside, blade height ×0.5 at the face → 1 at 1.2 mm out, → 0 within 0.8 mm inside (scaled at the blade tip).
  8. Seat rocks — seat_rocks :138 per rock: z₀ = q₀.₃₅(H₀ under footprint) − 0.3; grass top = q₀.₇₅(T) on a 1.5 mm dilated ring; raise by shortfall to max(1.5 mm, 0.45·height) above it, capped 1.5 mm; plinth = rock's own section at 0.15 mm extruded straight down to 0.3 mm below the lowest ground under the footprint (no downhill gap).
  9. Union — attach_base :181: (grass ∪ slab).simplify(0.005) ∪ rocks ∪ DB base; keep largest body; gl.stl_safe; export; stats incl. per-rock show/height/lift, deepest plinth, bare % (incl./visible), overhang.

B2. What makes it look good / rulings

  • Rocks are obstacles not soil: grass presses against stones (no moat, M2), parts upstream and rejoins behind (M4 ploughing avoided by the flow wrap).
  • Minority one-sided aprons (wake/scour) read as hydrology, not rings.
  • Rocks bedded (low-quantile seat + plinth) but guaranteed to emerge above grass (M5 swallowed rock fixed at v1e).
  • Grass lowered beside rocks laps the foot instead of heaping over it.
  • Shawn: "having each rock in a big bare area is kind of weird" (v1b); "stop that scaling thing; let the 20% be underneath rocks too" (v1d). Rejected: moats, size-scaled patches (bald field M3), min-height seating.

B3. Printability

Inherits grasslab's (stroke blades, slab box, stl_safe, keep largest) and rocklab's (facet/overhang rules). Own: plinth closes downhill gaps; emergence lift bounded; union order (simplify grass before rocks) fixed for determinism. Seeds 1–6 export one watertight body; seed 2 had a zero-volume rock/grass fin that stl_safe drops. v3 seed 1: 2,905 blades, 1.58 M faces, 79 MB, ≈ 85 s, bare 20.8% (15.7% visible), overhang 13.0 mm².

B4. Parameters (meadowlab.py :37–59)

ROCK_SEED_OFFSET 1000003, ROCK_PLACEMENTS_2X2 (6,9), FREE_LENS_POOL_PER_SQ (8,8), BARE_EARTH_FRAC 0.20, BARE_SAMPLE_STRIDE 4, APRON_PROB 0.35, APRON_WAKE_PROB 0.6, APRON_HALF_W (0.5,0.9)·R, APRON_STRETCH (1.2,1.9), APRON_OFFSET (0.6,1.0)·R, FOOTPRINT_Z_MM 0.45, TUCK_MM 0.5, SIDE_LOW 0.5, SIDE_MM 1.2, FLOW_RING_MM 0.6, ROCK_SINK_MM 0.3, ROCK_SEAT_Q 0.35, ROCK_RING_MM 1.5, ROCK_RING_Q 0.75, ROCK_SHOW_MM 1.5, ROCK_SHOW_FRAC 0.45, ROCK_RAISE_MAX_MM 1.5, PLINTH_TOP_MM 0.1. CLI --seed --cols --rows --check.

B5. Representations & libraries

Same as grasslab + manifold3d slice/to_polygons/extrude for footprints and plinths, matplotlib Path for rasterising polygons, rocklab's Manifold rocks. Masks and quantile statistics on the shared 0.12 mm grid.

B6. Mechanisms

Mechanism Where Class
Composition of labs via module import + hook args whole file G (= layer composition with shared state)
Independent RNG streams per subsystem :216 G → derived seeds
Solid → footprint mask (slice + rasterise) footprint_masks G (obstacle_mask stamp, same as library)
Obstacle-relative satellite shapes (aprons, wake/scour) apron_shapes G ("placement relative to a placed object + flow")
Coverage-target fill by adding natural-size items fill_bare_earth G (budgeted greedy scatter)
Obstacle → flow wrap via SDF lens rock_circles G
Blocker with tuck/side falloff on another layer build_grass hook G (inter-layer obstacle contract)
Seat on quantile + emergence constraint + plinth to lowest ground seat_rocks G (seating op for any object on terrain)
Base attach / keep-largest attach_base G (dup of grasslab's)

B7. Generalisation

meadowlab is the clearest argument for the declarative design: almost all its code is glue that a composition engine should provide: - Placement layers declare footprint, wrap, blocker, and seat behaviour; later layers (grass) automatically consume earlier layers' obstacle fields (exactly the library's ordered Region.layers contract, but richer: wrap + tuck + side falloff instead of just obstacle_mask). - Coverage targets (bare_earth ≥ 20% incl. under rocks) as a declarative constraint satisfied by a generic budgeted greedy filler. - Satellite shapes: apron(of=rocks, prob 0.35, side=wake 0.6|scour) as a parameterised definition referencing another definition's instances and the flow field. - Seating constraints (seat_quantile 0.35, emerge ≥ max(1.5, 0.45 h) above layer T, plinth_to=min_ground − 0.3) as declarative properties of any placed solid. - Per-definition derived seeds remove the stream interleaving and the U(8,8) alignment hack.
